# Handoff: where Mow And Grow is (7 Nov 2026)

For the next person (and their Claude) picking this up. Read `CLAUDE.md` first for the rules, then this.

## Where everything is

- **Repo:** `raynine2/Mow-And-Grow`. Working branch: **`claude/nice-rubin-9w0359`**. It holds all the work
  below, and it includes the teammate's Studio work: pets, eggs, guns, night, classes, lobby kit and product ids
  were merged in commit `a4d8452`.
- **Other branches:**
  - `studio-rescue-2026-10-26`: the Studio-only boss work, byte-for-byte; merged in `9b78791`. Keep it.
  - `studio-rescue-2026-10-27`: Studio's scripts byte-for-byte through 27 Oct 18:15 (class perks, FxPresets as a
    script, RarityFx, ClassFx, Settings panel, WelcomeBack, IntroCrate, Offline, TAB opens the menu, lower fists and
    all of 27 Oct's work); merged into the working branch with the owner's OK (27 Oct). Keep it.
  - `studio-snapshot-2026-10-21` and `studio-backup-2026-10-21` are snapshots of the Studio scripts from 21 Oct.
  - They are already merged into the working branch. Keep them, don't delete them.
- **The Studio place:** "RayanPoepHoof's Place: 10182026_1" (placeId 86945160031996). It is synced by hand from
  the branch, not through Rojo's live sync (see "Studio vs. git" below).
- **Backups inside the place:** `ServerStorage.MowALawnBackups.<name>`. Every time scripts or meshes were
  replaced, the old copies went here first:
  - `pre_chopfeel_22oct`
  - `pre_stalk_clumps_22oct` (the old clump meshes)
  - `pre_intro_22oct` (the old fly-over intro)
- **Code layout** (also in `README.md`):
  - `src/shared/Config.luau`: all tuning, sounds, zones, tools, daily rewards.
  - `src/server`: the server side, with RemoteEvents in `Net.luau`.
  - `src/client`: everything you see and hear.
- **Blender models:**
  - Source: `tools/blender/voxel/` (`models.py`, `build_clumps.py`).
  - Exported: `assets/models/MowClumps.fbx` plus its atlas.
  - Sizes: `src/shared/ModelSizes.luau`.

| `8802149`..`1b40b2f` | **7 Nov ~03:30–05:00, the boss finisher and the finale's grab-and-slam (owner sent a TikTok of a Roblox fight animation: "Kan je ook zo animated?", then picked "Films en boss finish").** New `client/Finisher` (on Puppet + Film + AnimeFx + CelFx): when a boss dies, everyone near it sees THEIR OWN avatar (R15; a copy, the real characters hidden) dash in, grab its chest (a face close-up from its chest), heave it into the sky (a low angle up), leap after it and hammer it down from a slow-motion hang (a red impact frame), land on it and pound it three times, one last punch (a black impact frame, a hit-stop), hop off in a hero landing and walk at the lens while it bursts behind them; ~6.7 s, eight hard-cut shots, SKIP ends it. Chopping (`HitFx`, `Fx`): your hits on a clump climb the major scale in tune (±1% jitter, was ±5%: a semitone is 6%), a long chop rocks sol-la, and the cut lands one note above the last hit (`HitFx.cutNote`; mowers and auto tools add no note). Picking up (`Hud`): the bundle that fills the bag plays three bloops up to the octave, the bag pops, glints and says FULL. Selling (`SellFx`): each bundle into the hopper a step higher, the coins ring up a pentatonic run from the start of each sale (it used to slide up 12 steps and wrap to the bottom mid-sale) on a steady 0.055 s beat (they land in bunches), the last coin rings the octave. Checked in Studio by logging every sound's pitch in a run: do re mi fa sol la on hits, re on a one-hit cut, the bag-full arpeggio, the sale's chips and coin run. All 15 pips = MASTERED: the page turns gold (title style), crown, banner, confetti, the server hears it; `SkillTrees.isMastered` / `onMastered` for the maxed-weapon ability to come. Window `client/SkillTreeWindow` (GemUpgrades' look: weapon pages with arrows and a weapon strip, 3 cards with pips, a details card with BUY, LOCKED for weapons never owned), opened by K or a SKILLS badge the module adds to Hud's side column (beside the SETTINGS gear on touch screens; Hud unchanged). One-line hooks: `Config` (SKILL_TREES), `Net` ("Skill"), `Main` (start, snapshot `skills`), `Tools.equipped` (the held tool with its skills), `Run` onCut (mastery, Harvest), `client/Input` (swing cooldown x the tool's SkillCooldown), `client/Client` (require). | `7614e2c` | **Phone pass (owner, 28 Oct 21:50: "make the whole game good on a phone ... the buttons, the text size, the films and above all the FPS; the grass isn't optimised for phones: point 15 of the report was never built; the UI must have perfect proportions").** Measured in Studio first: a run streams ~25,700 parts to a client, ~820 draw calls; each voxel clump mesh is ~1,900 triangles (counted from `assets/models/MowClumps.fbx`), so a run's ~700 clumps are ~1.3M triangles, most of the scene. **#15, `client/GrassLod` (new):** on phones/tablets (`PhonePerf.on`) and in Potato Mode only the clumps within ~70 studs of you or the camera are the real mesh; further out each is a 3-block stand-in in its own colours (stalk, bottom and top of the tuft, measured from the meshes; 36 triangles), switched per 48-stud square, a few dozen a frame. Only the mesh leaves the world (on that screen): the clump's model, collider and attributes stay, so Wind/Coach/AreaGoals still find it; a clump being cut (ClumpCarve) and the last glowing clumps (`LastClump` attribute from AreaGoals) stay real, and films hold everything real (`IntroCast.hideCharacters` -> `GrassLod.hold`). | _this commit_ | **Camera kick: "the screen moves when you swing"** (the report "Making Mow A Lawn feel as satisfying as Chop A Forest", 28 Oct, sections 3-4; tuning in the new `Config.CAMERA_KICK`). `client/CameraView`: `jolt(pitch, roll)` dips and rolls the view on a hit and springs back in ~0.15 s; `trauma(amount)` is a smooth rumble (trauma squared on noise, drains 1.8/s); `impact(amount)` = a bigger jolt + rumble for big moments; the old `shake(power, time)` now maps onto trauma (10 = 1) instead of jumping the camera to a random spot every frame. The tilt is taken back off just before Roblox's camera updates (`MowALawnCameraUndo`), so the aim never drifts. `client/HitFx`: every hand-tool hit jolts 0.75 deg (alternating roll); **no zoom-punch and no freeze on normal hits** (report: big hits only; the hand still bounces back); auto tools and mowers get a tiny bump at most every 0.3 s, and a mower's cut no longer zoom-punches and freezes on every clump; a clump you chopped falling, a gnome find and an area at 100% get `impact`, boss hits extra trauma. Phone/gamepad buzz (`CameraView.buzz`, HapticService, quietly does nothing where unsupported) only on gnome finds, rare reveals and area clears. **Settings: "Reduce camera shake"** (off by default; everything x0.35; saved in `data.alerts.calmCamera` from the lobby and now also from runs: `server/Run` has a "Setting" handler). Potato Mode halves the kicks and drops the zoom-punches. Play-tested with measured frames: hit 0.75 deg gone in ~0.17 s, chopped clump falling 1.3 deg + zoom-punch, gnome 1.15 deg, mower cut 0.18 deg bump, reduced 0.26 deg; 20 hits in a row peak 0.73 deg and the aim ends exactly where it started; the setting reaches the server. Synced to Studio (checksums match). |

| `6f360f2` | **Stalk clumps.** The grass clumps have a 3×3 stalk you chop like a tree trunk. The new meshes come from `models.py stalk_clump`. Server side, `Field.luau` adds an invisible `StalkCollider` and a `Stalk` attribute. The FBX is imported and swapped into `ServerStorage.VoxelModels` in Studio. |
| `60e9566` | **Chopping like Chop A Forest:**<br>• bigger, closer arms with wide fast swings (`ViewModel.luau`)<br>• chunks and slash marks that fly at the camera (`HitFx.luau`, `FxPresets.json`)<br>• calmer camera: less bob, no lean (`CameraView.luau`)<br>• a "TIMBER!" word when a clump falls |
| `cec1ffe` | **Daily reward streak.** A 7-day ladder `{50,75,100,150,200,300,600}` gems (`Config.DAILY_STREAK`) with a calendar window and a claim moment. Logic in `server/Daily.luau`. |
| `3246f81`, `b854d22` | **First-run coach** (`client/Coach.luau`). The steps are punch, collect, fill bag, sell and upgrade, each with a glow and a dotted trail to the target. |
| `fd42c2e` | **Big moments** (`client/Moments.luau`: rare grass reveal, area-cleared card) and a **soundscape** (`client/Soundscape.luau`: footsteps by material, birds and wind). |
| `a4d8452` | **Merge** of the teammate's Studio work with the generated world. |
| `f22e50a`, `72a609b` | **Chopping feel overhaul** and a smooth clump fall: it hinges on the near edge, and a ghost copy falls so you never see the underside. |

All of it passes `tools/compile-check.luau` and `rojo build`. Everything up to `ea61399` is in the Studio place
and was playtested there with no script errors.

the workspace attribute `FilmPlaying`, which nothing set: `Film.start/stop` set it now); the shed's bricks hung in

pose blend begun just before the hold doesn't finish (the film's clock is stopped): finish it by hand to judge a frame.

Earlier, some teammate work existed only in Studio and was almost overwritten. It was rescued and merged. So,
before syncing or using Rojo:
- make sure the branch has everyone's latest work, and save a backup of the place (File → Save to File As);
- if you edit scripts directly in Studio, commit them to the branch soon, or tell the other person.
